\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{16\kappa}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+2\left(\frac{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mu}+2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\right)L^{2}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+16\kappa^{3}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{B}.

∎

C.1.1
Proof of Theorem
1

Proof Sketch.

Before delving into the detailed proof, we first provide a sketch. Unlike the classic nonconvex-strongly-concave analysis, we consider the following potential function:

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})]+O\left(\kappa\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{4}\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mu}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\right)\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+O(\kappa\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+O(\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

Then, we derive the convergence of dual gap
\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}
:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{1}{16\kappa}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+2\left(\frac{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mu}+2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\right)L^{2}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+16\kappa^{3}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{B},

where
\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}
is the error of inner function estimation, and can be bounded using standard result from
[
1
]
. Combining above ingredients will yield

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{(\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{3}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L)(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+O\left(\kappa^{2}L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+O\left(\kappa\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{B}\right).

Unrolling above recursion will yield desired result.

Proof.

First due to Lemma
3
we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}[\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})]-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}-{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L(G_{f}^{2}\sigma^{2}+G_{g}^{2}\sigma^{2})}{B}.

Define potential function
\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})={\mathbb{E}}[\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})]+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+C_{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}
, where
C_{1},C_{2}
and
C_{3}
are determined later. Hence we have :

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}-{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L(G_{f}^{2}\sigma^{2}+G_{g}^{2}\sigma^{2})}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+C_{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-C_{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Since
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\leq\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\frac{2(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}
, we have

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}-{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L}-C_{3}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2C_{3}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{f}^{2}\sigma^{2}+G_{g}^{2}\sigma^{2})}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{1}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L(G_{f}^{2}\sigma^{2}+G_{g}^{2}\sigma^{2})}{B}

\displaystyle\quad-C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-C_{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

We plug in the bound for
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}
and
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}
:

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}-{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L}-C_{3}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{(2C_{3}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L)(G_{f}^{2}\sigma^{2}+G_{g}^{2}\sigma^{2})}{B}

\displaystyle+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{1}\right)\left((1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

\displaystyle+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{16\kappa}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+2\left(\frac{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mu}+2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\right)L^{2}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+16\kappa^{3}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

\displaystyle-C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-C_{3}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{4}-C_{3}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{(2C_{3}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L)(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}+C_{1}\right)2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right)\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+\left[\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{1}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}+C_{2}\right)2\left(\frac{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mu}+2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\right)L^{2}_{f}-C_{1}\right]{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{16\kappa}\right)-C_{2}\right]{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\left[16\kappa^{3}\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right)-C_{3}\right]{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2},

where at second inequality we use the fact
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\frac{1}{4\kappa L}
, so
\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\kappa L\geq\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{4}
.
We choose
C_{1},C_{2},C_{3}
such that the following conditions are satisfied:

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{4}-C_{3}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\geq 0,

\displaystyle\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{1}\right)(1-\beta)+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right)2\left(\frac{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mu}+2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\right)L^{2}_{f}-C_{1}\leq 0,

\displaystyle\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{16\kappa}\right)-C_{2}\leq 0,

\displaystyle 16\kappa^{3}\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right)-C_{3}\leq 0.

It suffices to guarantee the following inequality holding:

\displaystyle 16\kappa\left(1-\frac{1}{16\kappa}\right)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\leq C_{2}\leq\frac{1}{16\kappa^{3}}C_{3}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2},

\displaystyle C_{1}\geq\frac{1}{\beta}\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{2}\right)2\left(\frac{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mu}+2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\right)L^{2}_{f}+\frac{1-\beta}{\beta}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}.

A set of choices that can satisfy above conditions are the following:

\displaystyle\beta=\frac{1}{2},C_{1}=80\kappa\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{4}\left(\frac{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mu}+2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\right)+{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f},

\displaystyle C_{2}=16\kappa\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2},\ C_{3}=320\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}.

We also need
C_{3}=320\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}
, which can be satisfied by choosing
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}=\frac{1}{36\kappa^{2}L}
.

Hence we have:

\displaystyle\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(640\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{3}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L)(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left({\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}+C_{1}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+17\kappa\eta_{\mathbf{x}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Notice that
L\geq\max\left\{G_{g}L_{f},L_{f}\right\}
, and we choose
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\frac{1}{L}
, we know
C_{1}\leq 320\kappa\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\kappa L^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}\leq 321\kappa^{2}L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}
.
Summing over
t=0
to
T
and re-arranging terms yield:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{2(\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{0})-\Psi(\mathbf{x}^{T}))}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}+\frac{2(640\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{3}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L)(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}B}+642\kappa^{2}L^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+17\kappa\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{E[\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{0})]+321\kappa^{2}L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{-1})-\mathbf{z}^{0}\right\|^{2}+16\kappa\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{-1})-\mathbf{y}^{-1}\right\|^{2}+320\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{0}-\mathbf{x}^{-1}\right\|^{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2(640\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{3}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L)(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}B}+642\kappa^{2}L^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+17\kappa\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}.

By convention, we set
\mathbf{x}^{-1}=\mathbf{x}^{0}
, and with our choice
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}=\frac{1}{36\kappa^{2}L}
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{E[\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{0})-\min_{\mathbf{x}}\Phi(\mathbf{x})]+\kappa{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{0})-\mathbf{z}^{0}\right\|^{2}+LD_{\mathcal{Y}}^{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{(\kappa^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{3}L^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\kappa L)(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}B}+\kappa^{2}L\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\kappa\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right).

Due to our initialization,
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{0})-\mathbf{z}^{0}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\kappa}
, and this can be done by
O(\kappa^{2})
zero order sampling.
We choose
B=\Theta\left(\max\left\{\frac{\kappa^{2}L\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}},1\right\}\right)
,
T=O\left(\max\left\{\frac{\kappa^{2}L\Delta_{\Phi}}{\epsilon^{2}},\frac{\kappa^{2}L^{2}D_{\mathcal{Y}}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\}\right)
, which yields final gradient complexity of

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}(\Delta_{\Phi}+L^{2}D_{\mathcal{Y}})}{\epsilon^{2}}\max\left\{\frac{\kappa^{2}L\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}},1\right\}\right),

as desired.
∎

C.2
Proof of Nonconvex-concave setting

Proof Sketch:

Before delving into the detailed proof, we first provide a sketch.
In nonconvex-concave setting, we consider the following potential function:

\displaystyle\Psi^{t}=\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|

(3)

where
C_{1}=\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2(2\beta-\beta^{2})}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}
and
C_{2}=\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathbf{y}}}{\beta}
. Another key step in nonconvex-concave analysis is to bound primal function gap. During the dynamic of our algorithm, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\sqrt{2G_{h}^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}({\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle+LD_{\mathcal{Y}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|+{\mathbb{E}}\left[F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right].

Then we can derive that our algorithm satisfies the following descent inequality:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{t}-\Psi^{t-1}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\mathbb{E}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})]\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L(2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})

\displaystyle\quad+C_{1}\left(8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+C_{2}\sqrt{8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}}.

Lemma 5
.

For CODA-Primal (Algorithm
1
), under same assumptions as in Theorem
2
, the following statement holds :

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}({\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}.

Proof.

Let
\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}=\arg\min_{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Phi(\mathbf{x})+L\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}
. Notice that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+L\mathbb{E}\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+L(\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t},\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\rangle+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}).

Due to the boundedness of gradients, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]

\displaystyle+L(\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\langle\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})+\nabla h(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\rangle+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+L(\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\langle\nabla_{x}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\rangle+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}))

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\langle\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\rangle.

According to smoothness of
F(\cdot,\mathbf{y})
, we have:

\displaystyle\langle\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-{\mathbf{x}}_{t-1},\nabla_{x}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})+\frac{L}{2}\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})+L\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\frac{L}{2}\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})+L\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\frac{L}{2}\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\frac{L}{2}\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2},

where last step is due to that
\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}
is the minimizer of
\Phi(\cdot)+L\left\|\cdot-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}
.
So we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+L\mathbb{E}\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+L\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\frac{L}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}\right)+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\left(\frac{1}{2L}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+\frac{L}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}({\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}.

Finally using the fact that
\left\|\hat{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|=\left\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|/2L
concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 6
.

For CODA-Primal (Algorithm
1
), under the same assumptions made in Theorem
2
, if we choose
\eta\leq 1/4L
the following statement holds for any
\mathbf{y}\in\mathcal{Y}
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}({\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}{\sigma^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{f}D_{\mathcal{Y}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|.

Proof.

According to updating rule of
\mathbf{y}
:

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}^{t}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t-1}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{g}_{y}^{t-1}\right)=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t-1}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}(\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\zeta^{t-1})-\nabla r(\mathbf{y}^{t-1}))\right).

We define

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}_{t-1}

\displaystyle=(\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\zeta^{t-1})-\nabla r(\mathbf{y}^{t-1}))-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})

\displaystyle=\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\zeta^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),

and re-write the updating rule as:

\displaystyle\mathbf{y}^{t}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t-1}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})+\eta{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\right).

Due to the property of projection we have:

\displaystyle(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t})^{\top}(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}{\varepsilon}_{t-1})\geq 0.

Using the identity that
\langle\bm{a},\bm{b}\rangle=\frac{1}{2}(\|\bm{a}+\bm{b}\|^{2}-\|\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{b}\|^{2})
we have:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}+2\langle\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y},\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+2\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle-2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\langle\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle+2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle.

Due to
L
smoothness and concavity, we have

\displaystyle 2\langle\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y},\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle+2\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}(f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}))+{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

So we have:

\displaystyle f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y})-f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}\right)\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\langle\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle+\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}\right)\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\langle\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\zeta^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\zeta^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}\right)\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\langle\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\zeta^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\zeta^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle.

Now we take expectation over the randomness of
\zeta^{t-1}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y})-f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}({\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-{\mathbb{E}}\langle\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\zeta^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbb{E}}\langle\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1},\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}({\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}{\sigma^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{f}D_{\mathcal{Y}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|.

∎

Lemma 7
.

Define potential function:

\displaystyle\Psi^{t}=\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|

(4)

where
C_{1}=\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2(2\beta-\beta^{2})}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}
and
C_{2}=\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathbf{y}}}{\beta}
. Then the following statement holds:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{t}-\Psi^{t-1}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\mathbb{E}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})]\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L(2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})

\displaystyle\quad+C_{1}\left(8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+C_{2}\sqrt{8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}}.

Proof.

Recalling Lemma
5
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}({\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}.

Following
[
19
]
, we split
\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\underbrace{F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}))-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s}))}_{A}+\underbrace{F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s}))-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t})}_{B}+\underbrace{F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})}_{C}\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}\left[F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right]

for some iteration
s
.

For A:

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}))-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s}))

\displaystyle\leq F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}))-F(\mathbf{x}^{s},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}))+F(\mathbf{x}^{s},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s}))-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s}))

\displaystyle\leq 2(t-1-s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\sqrt{2G_{h}^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}.

For B, we evoke Lemma
6
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s}))-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t})]\leq\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}({\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+LD_{\mathcal{Y}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|.

For C:

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\leq\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\sqrt{2G_{h}^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}.

Putting pieces together yields:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\sqrt{2G_{h}^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}({\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2})+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+LD_{\mathcal{Y}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}\left[F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right].

Notice that
\sqrt{2G_{h}^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}\leq 2(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})

Plugging above bound back to Lemma
5
yields:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})]\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}({\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]

\displaystyle+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left((2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}2(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+LD_{\mathcal{Y}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt\hskip 18.49988pt\left.+{\mathbb{E}}\left[F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right]\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}({\mathbf{x}}^{t-1})]+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left((2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}2(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+\frac{\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}).

Recalling the definition of
\Psi^{t}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{t}-\Psi^{t-1}]=\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+C_{1}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}+C_{2}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}}-\left(C_{1}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+C_{2}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left((2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}2(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+\frac{\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+C_{1}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}+C_{2}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}}-\left(C_{1}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+C_{2}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}-C_{1}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathcal{Y}}-C_{2}\right)\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left((2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}2(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+\frac{\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+C_{1}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}+C_{2}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}}.

Evoking Lemma
2
to replace
\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}
yields:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{t}-\Psi^{t-1}]\leq\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}-C_{1}\right)\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathcal{Y}}-C_{2}\right)\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\ +2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left((2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}2(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+\frac{\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+C_{1}\left((1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+C_{2}\sqrt{(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\left(\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}-C_{1}+(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}\right)}_{\clubsuit}\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+\underbrace{\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathcal{Y}}-C_{2}+(1-\beta)C_{2}\right)}_{\heartsuit}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\ +2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\mathbb{E}\left((2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}2(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+\frac{\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+C_{1}\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+C_{2}\sqrt{4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}}

Since we choose
C_{1}=\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2(2\beta-\beta^{2})}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}
and
C_{2}=\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathbf{y}}}{\beta}
, we know
\clubsuit=0
and
\heartsuit=0
. Hence we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{t}-\Psi^{t-1}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\mathbb{E}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})]\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}+2L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L(2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})

\displaystyle\quad+C_{1}\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+C_{2}\sqrt{4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}}

which concludes the proof.
∎

C.2.1
Proof of Theorem
2

We first evoke Lemma
7
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}

\displaystyle(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\|^{2}\leq\frac{8{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{t-1}-\Psi^{t}]}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}

\displaystyle+16L\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{s})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\mathbb{E}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})]\right)

\displaystyle+16L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+16L(2t-1-2s)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+16\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+\frac{8C_{1}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+\frac{8C_{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\sqrt{8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}}.

We sum above inequality for
t=jS
to
(j+1)S-1
, with
\mathbf{x}^{s}=\mathbf{x}^{jS}
:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=jS}^{(j+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{t=jS}^{(j+1)S-1}\left[\frac{8{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{t}-\Psi^{t+1}]}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+16L\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{jS})-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{jS})-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\mathbb{E}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})]\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+16SL\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+\sum_{t=jS}^{(j+1)S-1}16L(2t+1-2jS)\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+16S\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+8SC_{1}\left(8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+8SC_{2}\sqrt{8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{jS}-\Psi^{(j+1)S)}]}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}

\displaystyle\quad+16L\left(\frac{\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{jS})-\mathbf{y}^{jS}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}^{jS})-\mathbf{y}^{(j+1)S}\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\mathbb{E}[F(\mathbf{x}^{(j+1)S},\mathbf{y}^{(j+1)S})-F(\mathbf{x}^{jS},\mathbf{y}^{jS})]\right)

\displaystyle\quad+16SL\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+32S^{2}L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+16S\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+8S\frac{C_{1}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)+8S\frac{C_{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\sqrt{8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}}.

Now we further sum over
j=0
to
(T+1)/S-1

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{j=0}^{(T+1)/S-1}\sum_{t=jS}^{(j+1)S-1}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}^{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T+1}\frac{8{\mathbb{E}}[\Psi^{0}-\Psi^{T}]}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+{16L}\left(\frac{D^{2}_{\mathcal{Y}}}{2S\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{T},\mathbf{y}^{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{0})]}{T+1}\right)

\displaystyle+16L\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+32SL\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+16\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+8\frac{C_{1}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)+8\frac{C_{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\sqrt{8(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{h}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2})+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}},

where
C_{1}=\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2(2\beta-\beta^{2})}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}
and
C_{2}=\frac{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}D_{\mathbf{y}}}{\beta}
. Notice that
{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{T},\mathbf{y}^{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{0})]\leq{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{T},\mathbf{y}^{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{T})+F(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{0})]\leq T\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})+\Delta_{\Phi}
.

Now, We choose
S=\sqrt{\frac{D^{2}_{\mathcal{Y}}}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})}}
,
\eta_{\mathbf{y}}=\Theta\left(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{L\sigma^{2}}\right)
and
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}=\Theta\left(\frac{\epsilon^{6}}{LD_{\mathcal{Y}}\sigma^{2}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})}\right)
,
B=\Theta\left(1\right)
,
M=\Theta\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}L^{4}D^{2}_{\mathcal{Y}}}{\epsilon^{4}}\right)
and
T=\Theta(\max\left\{\frac{\Delta_{\hat{\Phi}}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\epsilon^{2}},\frac{L\Delta_{\Phi}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\})
, which yields total gradient complexity of

\displaystyle O\left(\max\left\{\frac{L^{3}\sigma^{2}D^{2}_{\mathcal{Y}}(G_{h}+G_{f}G_{g})\Delta_{\hat{\Phi}}}{\epsilon^{8}},\frac{L\Delta_{\Phi}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\}\right).

Appendix D
Proof of Dual Composition Setting

In this section we provide the proof of results in primal compostion setting (Theorem
3
and Theorem
4
).

Proof Sketch:

Before delving into the detailed proof, we first provide a sketch. We construct the following two-level potential function:

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{t+1}:=

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{96G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

where
s^{t+1}:=-\frac{2}{\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}\mu}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}
and

\displaystyle\tilde{F}^{t+1}:=\hat{F}^{t+1}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}
is the iterate difference of our auxilliary variables, and it can be controlled by

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+4\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)G_{g}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+2\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Putting pieces together we can derive the following descent property:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}},\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right\}\cdot{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla G(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-O\left(\eta_{\mathbf{y}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-O\left(\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\beta^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+\beta^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}.

Unrolling above recursion will give desired rate. The converegcne of merely-convex-nonconcave setting follows similar technique.

D.1
Proof of Strongly-convex-nonconcave setting

In nonconcave setting, besides the Lemma
2
, we also need the following bound on tracking error between two iterates.

Lemma 8

(Convergence of Iterates difference)
.

For Algorithm
2
, under the assumptions of Theorem
3
, the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+4\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)G_{g}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}\left(3G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+6\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

Proof.

For the ease of presentation, we define the following two auxiliary variables:

\displaystyle g^{t}=\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\xi\in\mathcal{M}^{t}}g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi),\ g^{t\mapsto t-1}=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{\xi\in\mathcal{M}^{t}}\left(g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi)-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\xi)\right).

According to updating rule of
\mathbf{z}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}=(1-\beta)(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1})+(1-\beta)(g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2})+\beta(g^{t}-g^{t-1}).

Taking expectation w.r.t.
\mathcal{M}^{t}
, and
\mathcal{M}^{t-1}
yields:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|(1-\beta)(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1})+(1-\beta)(g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2})+\beta(g^{t}-g^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{2-2\beta}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}+(g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2})\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2-2\beta}{\beta}\right)\left\|\beta(g^{t}-g^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)(1-\beta){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}+(g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2})\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t}-g^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)(1-\beta)\left(1+\frac{\beta}{2-2\beta}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)(1-\beta)\left(1+\frac{2-2\beta}{\beta}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t}-g^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\underbrace{\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2}\right\|^{2}}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t}-g^{t-1}\right\|^{2}}_{T_{2}}

where in the first and third steps we use Young’s inequality that
\left\|\mathbf{a}+\mathbf{b}\right\|^{2}\leq(1+a)\left\|\mathbf{a}\right\|^{2}+(1+\frac{1}{a})\left\|\mathbf{b}\right\|^{2}
.
Now we bound
T_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle T_{1}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{\xi^{t}\in\mathcal{M}^{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi^{t})-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\xi^{t})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+2\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\left(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{\xi^{t-1}\in\mathcal{M}^{t-1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\xi^{t-1})-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-2};\xi^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)G_{g}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}\right),

and for
T_{2}
:

\displaystyle T_{2}\leq\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}\left(3G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+6\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

Putting pieces together will conclude the proof.
∎

The following Lemma establishes the difference of the exact gradient computed on
F
versus the gradients we actually used in Algorithm
2
.

Lemma 9

(Gradient difference)
.

For Algorithm
2
, under the assumptions of Theorem
3
, the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{4(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq 2L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+2\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Proof.

By the definition of
\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta^{t},\xi^{t})\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta^{t})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta^{t},\xi^{t})\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta^{t})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi^{t})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2},

where at the last step we use the bounded variance assumption. Similarly by the definition of
\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}
we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta^{t},\xi^{t})\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta^{t})-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\right\|^{2}\leq 2L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+2\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}.

∎

Lemma 10

(Connection between stationary measure and iterates)
.

For Algorithm
2
, under the assumptions of Theorem
3
, if we define

\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\mathbf{x}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right)\right),\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\mathbf{y}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right)\right)

,
then the following statement holds:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+4\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{8(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Proof.

We begin with proving the first statement. According to updating rule we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right)\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+4{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta^{t})-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+4{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+4\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B},

where at last step we apply Lemma
9
.
Similarly, for the second statement we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}+\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right)\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta^{t},\xi^{t})\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta^{t})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi^{t})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|+4{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta^{t},\xi^{t})\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta^{t})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi^{t})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+4{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{8(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

∎

The following lemma connects
F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
and
F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})
.

Lemma 11

(Dual Variable One Iteration Analysis)
.

For Algorithm
2
, under the assumptions of Theorem
3
, the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left({\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}2(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle-L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

By the optimality of projection and updating rule of
\mathbf{w}
:

\displaystyle\left\langle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}+(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\rangle\leq 0

Re-arranging terms we have:

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\rangle\leq-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

Adding and subtracting
\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})
yields:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\rangle\leq-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

Applying Cauchy-Schwartz inequality yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}\leq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

Now, we take expectation over the randomness of
\mathcal{B}^{t}
, and apply Lemma
9
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{2(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

Plugging in Lemma
2
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle++\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\frac{2(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left((1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

On the other hand, since
F
is
L
smooth, we have:

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})\geq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle-\frac{L}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad=\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle-\frac{L}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad\geq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle-\frac{L}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{L\sqrt{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|{L\sqrt{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad\geq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle-\frac{L}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\geq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle-\left(\frac{L}{2}+\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

Putting pieces together will conclude the proof:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left({\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}2(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle-L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

∎

The following lemma connects
F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
and
F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})
.

Lemma 12

(One Iteration Descent Lemma)
.

For Algorithm
2
, under the assumptions of Theorem
3
, the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})]\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

Proof.

According to property of projection we have:

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}+\frac{1}{\eta}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle\geq 0

(5)

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}+\frac{1}{\eta}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle\geq 0

(6)

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}+\frac{1}{\eta}(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle\geq 0

(7)

Since
F(\cdot,\mathbf{y})
is strongly convex for any
\mathbf{y}\in\mathcal{Y}
, we have:

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle+\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle+\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\langle\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle+\frac{\mu}{2}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\langle\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

where at second inequality we use the fact of (
7
) .
For the first dot product term, we observe that:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\Delta\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\|\Delta\|^{2}+\mu\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2},

(8)

where
\Delta=(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t})-(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}).

For the second dot product, we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta(1-\beta)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2},

where we plug in Lemma
9
at last step.

For the third dot product, we use the following identity:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\langle\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle=\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\Delta\|^{2}\right)

(9)

By putting pieces together we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}.

(10)

Recall Lemma
11
gives the following lower bound of
{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})]
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle-L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

(11)

Hence, Adding (
10
) and (
11
) yields:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})]\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right),

which concludes the proof.

∎

Lemma 13
.

For Algorithm
2
, under the assumptions of Theorem
3
, define the following auxillary function
\hat{F}^{t+1}

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{t+1}:=

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{168G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{84G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

where
s^{t+1}:=-\frac{2}{\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}\mu}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

then the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}+C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

where

\displaystyle C_{1}=\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}-4L_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}-\frac{168G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}-\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}},

(12)

\displaystyle C_{2}=\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{3}{2}\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}.

(13)

Proof.

Define
\Delta^{t+1}:=\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}-(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1})
. According to (
6
) and (
7
):

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle.

If we define
\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z})=\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z};\mathcal{B}^{t})+\nabla h(\mathbf{x})
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t};\xi^{t}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t};\xi^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}-(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\mu}{4}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\Delta^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\mu\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

where we use the Lipschitzness of
\nabla f(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z};\xi)
at the last step.
Since
\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle=\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\Delta^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\mu}{4}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}_{f}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\mu\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

Multiplying both sides with
\frac{4}{\mu\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\mu\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{2}{\mu\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

(14)

Recall that in Lemma
12
we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})]\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

Combining Lemma
12
together with (
24
) yields:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}

\displaystyle[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-(F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})+s^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)-\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

Now we plug in Lemma
8
to replace
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}
together with the fact that
\frac{1}{\beta}\geq 1
and get:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle+s^{t+1}-(F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})+s^{t})]
