\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(4L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}+\frac{84G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{\mu}{2}-\frac{5}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\mu+\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\cdot 18\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}.

Recall our definition of potential function
\hat{F}^{t+1}

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{t+1}:=

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{168G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{84G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

We conclude that:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]\geq\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}-4L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}-\frac{168G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}-\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{3}{2}\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

∎

Lemma 14
.

Let

be defined in (

) and (

(15)

\displaystyle 1-\left(2C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+1\right)(1-\beta)^{2}\geq 0,

then we have:

\displaystyle\quad-\left(4C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+2C_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4\beta^{2}C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4\beta^{2}C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}-\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M},

where

Proof.

where

\displaystyle-\left(4C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+2C_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4\beta^{2}C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4\beta^{2}C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}-\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(2C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(4C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+2C_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4\beta^{2}C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4\beta^{2}C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}-\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Now we plug in Lemma
2
and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}

\displaystyle-\tilde{F}^{t}]\geq\frac{C_{1}}{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-4(2C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+2C_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+1)(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{12}{\beta}+6\beta\right)G_{g}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1-\left(2C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+1\right)(1-\beta)^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(4C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+2C_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4\beta^{2}C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4\beta^{2}C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}-\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\eta_{\mathbf{y}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-4(2C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+2C_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+1)(1-\beta)^{2}G_{f}^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{12}{\beta}+6\beta\right)G_{g}^{2}\geq 0,

\displaystyle 1-\left(2C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+2C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+1\right)(1-\beta)^{2}\geq 0,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\frac{6}{\beta}G_{g}^{2}\geq 0.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\quad-\left(4C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+2C_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4\beta^{2}C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4\beta^{2}C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}-\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}.

Proof of Theorem
3

Evoking Lemma
14
and summing the inequality from
t=1
to
T
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]}{T}+\left(4C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+2C_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4\beta^{2}C_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4\beta^{2}C_{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\frac{C_{1}}{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{\frac{C_{1}}{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2},\frac{C_{2}}{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\right\}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

We compute the upper and lower bound of

and
C_{2}
. For
C_{1}

\displaystyle C_{1}=\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}-4L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}-\frac{168G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}-\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}

The upper bound
C_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}
holds trivially. For lower bound,
since we choose

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}=\min\left\{\frac{1}{20L},\frac{1}{40\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}},\frac{1}{10L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}},\frac{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}{3840G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}\right\}

we can conclude that
C_{1}\geq\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}
.

For
C_{2}
:

\displaystyle C_{2}=\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{3}{2}\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}.

The upper bound
C_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{3\mu}{2}
holds trivially. For lower bound, since we choose:

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{x}}=\min\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}L^{2}},\frac{\mu}{12L^{2}_{f}}\right\},\beta=0.1\leq 1-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}

it holds that
C_{2}\geq\frac{1}{3\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}
.

Since
\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\leq C_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}
and
\frac{1}{3\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\leq C_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{3\mu}{2}\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}
, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]}{T}+\left(\eta_{\mathbf{y}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\beta^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{\frac{1}{16}\eta_{\mathbf{y}},\frac{1}{6}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

We then need to verify the conditions
15
,
16
and
17
in Lemma
14
can hold under our choice of
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}
,
\eta_{\mathbf{y}}
and
\beta
. To guarantee (

) holding, we need:

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{64G_{f}^{2}(\eta_{x}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+4\eta_{x}L_{f}^{2}+1)},2\times 10^{-4}\times\frac{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}\right\}.

To guarantee condition (
16
) holding, we need

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\frac{23}{1700L_{f}^{2}},\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq\frac{23}{500L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}.

To guarantee condition (

) holding, we need:

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq\frac{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{14400L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}.

Next we examine how large the
{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]
is.
By definition of potential function, we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]

\displaystyle=\hat{F}^{T}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{T}-g(\mathbf{y}^{T-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\hat{F}^{0}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{y}^{-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-\mathbf{z}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{y}^{-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{\beta-\frac{\beta^{2}}{4}}\frac{4L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{0}-\mathbf{z}_{j}^{-1}\right\|^{2}.

By convention
\mathbf{x}^{-1}=\mathbf{x}^{0}
,
\mathbf{y}^{0}=\mathbf{y}^{-1}
, and our choice
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{0}-g(\mathbf{y}^{0})\right\|^{2}\leq O(1)
, , we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]\leq\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}+O(1).

Next we examine how large the
{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]
is.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]

\displaystyle=F(\mathbf{x}^{T},\mathbf{y}^{T})+s^{T}-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{168G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{T}-\mathbf{y}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{84G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{T-1}-\mathbf{y}^{T-2}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{T}-\mathbf{x}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-F(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{0})-s^{0}+\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{168G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{48G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{0}-\mathbf{y}^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{84G_{g}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{-1}-\mathbf{y}^{-2}\right\|^{2}-\left(\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{0}-\mathbf{x}^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq F_{\max}+\left(\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\mu-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}-\frac{2L_{f}^{2}}{\mu}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{T}-\mathbf{x}^{T-1}\right\|^{2},

where we used the convention
\mathbf{x}^{-1}=\mathbf{x}^{0}
and
\mathbf{y}^{-1}=\mathbf{y}^{-2}
. Notice that
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{T}-\mathbf{x}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}\leq\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{T-1},\mathbf{z}^{T};\mathcal{B}^{T-1})+\nabla h(\mathbf{x}^{T-1})\right\|^{2}\leq 2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})
,
we have
{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]\leq F_{\max}+7\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})+2\mu\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})
.

Using the fact that
\beta-\frac{\beta^{2}}{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\beta
when
\beta\leq 2
yield:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\|\nabla G(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\max\left\{\frac{16}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}},\frac{6}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right\}\cdot\frac{F_{\max}+7\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})+2\mu\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})+O(1)}{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\max\left\{\frac{16}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}},\frac{6}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right\}\cdot\left(\left(\eta_{\mathbf{y}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})+4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\beta^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+8\beta^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{72L_{f}^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{F_{\max}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})+\mu\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T}\right)+O\left(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\max\left\{\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B},\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right\}.

To guarantee RHS is less than
\epsilon^{2}
, we need:

T=O\left(\frac{F_{\max}}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\epsilon^{2}}\right)
, and

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\max\left\{\frac{\kappa^{3}L\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}1\right\}\right),B=\Theta\left(\max\left\{\frac{\kappa^{2}L_{f}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}},1\right\}\right),\beta=0.1,

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{1}{L^{2}},\frac{\mu}{L^{2}_{f}}\right\}\right),\eta_{\mathbf{y}}=\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{1}{L},\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}},\frac{1}{L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}},\frac{\mu^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}\right\}\right)

which yields the total gradient complexity:

\displaystyle O\left(\max\left\{\frac{\kappa^{2}L^{2}_{f}\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}},1\right\}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{3}F_{\max}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right).

D.3
Proof of Convex-nonconcave Setting

Lemma 15

(Connection between stationary measure and iterates)
.

For Algorithm
2
, under assumptions of Theorem
4
, if we define

\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y})=\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y})-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\alpha_{t}\mathbf{x}^{t}\right)\right),\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\mathbf{y}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})\right)\right)

,
then the following statement holds:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+8L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+8\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{8(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Proof.

We begin with proving the first statement. According to updating rule we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\alpha_{t}\mathbf{x}^{t}\right)\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\alpha_{t}\mathbf{x}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+8{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+8{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+8L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+8\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

where at last step we apply Lemma
9
.
Similarly, for the second statement we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}+\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right)\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\mathbf{y}^{t}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi)-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|+4{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi)-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+4{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{2}f(\mathbf{x}^{t},g(\mathbf{y}^{t}))\nabla g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{8(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

∎

Proposition 1
.

For Algorithm
2
, under assumptions of Theorem
4
, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}\left\|\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{L^{\prime}}{2}}{L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1}}\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\rangle}_{A}

\displaystyle+\frac{\frac{L^{\prime}}{2}+\alpha_{t-1}}{L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1}}\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\rangle}_{B}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}\left\|\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

where the last inequality is due to

(1)
F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})
is
L^{\prime}
smooth and hence
A\geq\frac{1}{L^{\prime}}\left\|\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}
; and

(2)
F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})
is
\alpha_{t-1}
strongly convex and
B\geq\alpha_{t-1}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}
,

∎

Lemma 16
.

For Algorithm
2
, under assumptions of Theorem
4
, then the following statement holds:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq-\left(\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t-1}({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})-\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

Proof.

According to updating rule:

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}+\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle\geq 0

(18)

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}+\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle\geq 0

(19)

\displaystyle\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}+\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle\geq 0.

(20)

Due to the
\alpha_{t}
-strong convexity of
F_{t}(\cdot,\mathbf{y})
, together with (
20
) we have

\displaystyle F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\langle\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle}_{A}-\underbrace{\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\langle\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle}_{B}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle}_{C}+\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

Similar to the proof of Lemma
12
, for A, we observe that:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle}_{\text{①}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\Delta\right\rangle}_{\text{②}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\rangle}_{\text{③}}

where
\Delta=(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t})-(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}).

For ①, recall the definition of
F_{t}
:

①

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{x}^{t}-\alpha_{t-1}\mathbf{x}^{t},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle+\left\langle\alpha_{t}\mathbf{x}^{t}-\alpha_{t-1}\mathbf{x}^{t},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}+\left(\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}\right)\left\langle\mathbf{x}^{t},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle-\left(\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t-1}-\alpha_{t}\right)(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t-1}-\alpha_{t}\right)\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}.

For ②, we use Cauchy-Schwartz:

\displaystyle\text{②}\geq-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\|\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\|\Delta\|^{2}.

For ③, we use Proposition
1
:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}\left\|\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\nabla F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\|^{2}

Since
\frac{1}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\geq 0
, putting pieces together yields:

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t-1}-\alpha_{t}\right)(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})+\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t-1}-\alpha_{t}\right)\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\|\Delta\|^{2}.

For B, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\langle\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle=\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\Delta\right\|^{2}.

For C, we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F_{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2},

where we plug in Lemma
9
at last step.

Putting lower bound of A, B and C together yields:

\displaystyle F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t-1}-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right)\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t-1}-\alpha_{t}\right)(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})

\displaystyle\quad-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Since
F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F_{t}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})=F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})+\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t}(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})
, and also recall in Lemma
11
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left({\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}2(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle-L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2},

we can conclude the proof:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq-\left(\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t-1}({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})-\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

∎

Lemma 17
.

For Algorithm
2
, under assumptions of Theorem
4
, define the following auxiliary function

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{t+1}:=

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{576G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2},

where
s^{t+1}:=-\frac{8}{\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}\alpha_{t+1}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

then the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq C_{1}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{2}^{t}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t+1}}-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{1152L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right),

where

\displaystyle C_{1}^{t}=\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}-4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}-\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}-\frac{576G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}},

(21)

\displaystyle C_{2}^{t}=\left(\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right).

(22)

Proof.

Define
\Delta^{t+1}:=\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}-(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1})
. According to (
6
) and (
7
):

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle.

If we define
\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z})=\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{z};\zeta^{t})+\nabla h(\mathbf{x})+\alpha_{t}\mathbf{x}
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\eta}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}-(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1},\,(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L_{f}^{2}}{2c_{t}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{c_{t}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}}{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\Delta^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t})-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

where we use Proposition
1
at the last step.
Since
\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\langle\Delta^{t+1},\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\rangle=\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\Delta^{t+1}\right\|^{2}
, and
\frac{1}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}-\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\geq 0
, and
L^{\prime}\geq\alpha_{t-1}

we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L_{f}^{2}}{2c_{t}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{c_{t}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}}{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{4}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Rearranging terms yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{L_{f}^{2}}{2c_{t}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{c_{t}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t}}{4}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2},

where we use the fact
\alpha_{t-1}\geq\alpha_{t}
.

Multiplying both sides with
\frac{16}{\alpha_{t}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{8}{\alpha_{t}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{8}{\alpha_{t}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(1-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

(23)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{8L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}c_{t}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{8c_{t}}{\alpha_{t}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

(24)

Recall the definition of
s^{t}
, and choose
c_{t}=\frac{\alpha_{t}}{8}
:

\displaystyle s^{t+1}-s^{t}\geq

\displaystyle-\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha^{2}_{t}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t+1}}-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}.

Recall that Lemma
16
gives:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq-\left(\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t-1}({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})-\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

Evoking Lemma
16
together with (
24
) yields:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-(F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})+s^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{2}-\frac{3}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}-\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{4}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}L^{\prime}}{2(L^{\prime}+\alpha_{t-1})}-\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t-1}(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})+\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t+1}}-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

Now we plug in Lemma
8
to replace
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}
together with the fact that
\frac{1}{\beta}\geq 1
and get:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-(F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})+s^{t})]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\|^{2}-\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{L^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{2}+\frac{768L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{576L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{2}-\frac{5}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{768L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{t-1}(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2})+\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t+1}}-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\cdot 18\beta\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

Recall our definition of potential function
\hat{F}^{t+1}

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{t+1}:=

\displaystyle F(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+s^{t+1}-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{576G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}.

We conclude that:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{t+1}-\hat{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}-4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}-\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}-\frac{576G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-(1-\beta)^{2}\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t+1}}-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}-\left(2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{1152L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

∎

Lemma 18
.

Let
C_{1},C_{2}
be defined in (
21
) and (
22
). If the following conditions hold:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{T}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\frac{6}{\beta}G_{g}^{2}\geq 0.

then for Algorithm

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{C^{t}_{1}}{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C^{t}_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}-2C^{t}_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}\alpha_{t}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}+4C^{t}_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}+4C^{t}_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})+2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}
