D.3.1
Proof Theorem
4

Evoking Lemma
18
and re-arranging terms yields:

\displaystyle\frac{C^{t}_{1}}{2}

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{C^{t}_{2}}{2}\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]-\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t+1}}-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}+4C^{t}_{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}+4C^{t}_{1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})+2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4C^{t}_{1}\beta^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+8C^{t}_{2}\beta^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{1152L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}.

We compute the upper and lower bound of
C^{t}_{1}
and
C^{t}_{2}
. For
C^{t}_{1}

\displaystyle C_{1}^{t}=\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\frac{L}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}-4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}-\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}-\frac{576G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t+1}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}.

The upper bound
C^{t}_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}
holds trivially. For lower bound,
since we choose

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq c\cdot\min\left\{\frac{1}{L},\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}},\frac{1}{L_{f}G_{g}^{2}},\frac{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}{G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}\right\},

for some
c\geq 0
,
we can choose sufficiently small
c
such that
C^{t}_{1}\geq\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}
.

For
C^{t}_{2}
:

\displaystyle C^{t}_{2}=\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{2}-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(1-\beta)^{2}}.

The upper bound
C^{t}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2}
holds trivially. For lower bound, since we choose:

\displaystyle\beta=0.1\leq 1-\frac{\sqrt{2}}{2}

it holds that
C^{t}_{2}\geq\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}
.

Since
\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\leq C^{t}_{1}\leq\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}
and
\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\leq C^{t}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}+\frac{\alpha_{t}}{2}\leq\frac{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}
, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}{16}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{4}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\leq{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{t+1}-\tilde{F}^{t}]-\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha_{t}-\alpha_{t-1}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{8}{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\frac{\alpha_{t}}{\alpha_{t+1}}-\frac{\alpha_{t-1}}{\alpha_{t}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}+8\eta_{\mathbf{x}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})+2{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2\beta^{2}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+16\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{1152L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}.

Summing the above inequality from
t=0
to
T-1
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]}{T}+\frac{\left(\alpha_{T-1}-\alpha_{0}\right)D^{2}_{\mathcal{X}}}{2T}

\displaystyle-\frac{8\left(\frac{\alpha_{T-1}}{\alpha_{T}}-\frac{\alpha_{0}}{\alpha_{1}}\right)D^{2}_{\mathcal{X}}}{T}+4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\alpha_{t}^{2}D_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}

\displaystyle+O\left(\left(\eta_{\mathbf{x}}+{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\min\left\{\frac{1}{16}\eta_{\mathbf{y}},\frac{1}{4}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\right\}\left(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\right).

We then need to verify the conditions
25
,
26
and
27
in Lemma
18
can hold under our choice of
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}
,
\eta_{\mathbf{y}}
and
\beta
. To guarantee (
25
) holding, we need:

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{1}{\eta_{x}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}},\frac{1}{L_{f}G_{g}^{2}},\frac{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}\right\}\right),\forall\ 0\leq t\leq T-1.

To guarantee condition (
26
) holding, we need

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\Theta\left(\frac{1}{L_{f}^{2}}\right),\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq\Theta\left(\frac{1}{L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}\right).

To guarantee condition (
27
) holding, we need:

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq\Theta\left(\frac{\alpha_{T}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}\right).

Next we examine how large the
{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]
is.
By definition of potential function, we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]

\displaystyle=\hat{F}^{T}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{T}-g(\mathbf{y}^{T-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\hat{F}^{0}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{y}^{-1})\right\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{1-(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-\mathbf{z}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{y}^{-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{(1-\frac{\beta}{2})^{2}}{\beta-\frac{\beta^{2}}{4}}\frac{64L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{t}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{0}-\mathbf{z}_{j}^{-1}\right\|^{2}

By convention
\mathbf{y}^{0}=\mathbf{y}^{-1}
,
\mathbf{z}^{0}=\mathbf{z}^{-1}
, and our choice
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{y}^{0})\right\|^{2}\leq O(1)
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\tilde{F}^{T}-\tilde{F}^{0}]\leq\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}+O(1).

Next we examine how large the
{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]
is.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]

\displaystyle=F(\mathbf{x}^{T},\mathbf{y}^{T})+s^{T}-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\alpha_{T}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{576G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha_{T}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{T}-\mathbf{y}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{a_{T}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{T-1}-\mathbf{y}^{T-2}\right\|^{2}+\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{T}-\mathbf{x}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\alpha_{T-1}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{T}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-F(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{0})-s^{0}+\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+4L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{4}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}L^{2}}{2}+\frac{768G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{a_{0}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}+\frac{576G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta}{a_{0}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{0}-\mathbf{y}^{-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{1}{8\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}+\frac{768G_{g}^{2}}{a_{0}^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\beta}\right)\left\|\mathbf{y}^{-1}-\mathbf{y}^{-2}\right\|^{2}-\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{0}-\mathbf{x}^{-1}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha_{0}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq F_{\max}+\frac{7}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{T}-\mathbf{x}^{T-1}\right\|^{2},

where we used the convention
\mathbf{x}^{-1}=\mathbf{x}^{0}
and
\mathbf{y}^{-1}=\mathbf{y}^{-2}
. Notice that
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{T}-\mathbf{x}^{T-1}\right\|^{2}\leq\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{T-1}}\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{x}^{T-1},\mathbf{z}^{T};\zeta)+\nabla h(\mathbf{x}^{T-1})\right\|^{2}\leq 2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}^{2}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})
,
we have
{\mathbb{E}}[\hat{F}^{T}-\hat{F}^{0}]\leq F_{\max}+7\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})
.

Due to our choice that
\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq 8\eta_{\mathbf{x}}
,
\alpha_{0}=...=\alpha_{T}=\alpha
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{F_{\max}+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}(G_{f}^{2}+G_{h}^{2})}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T}\right)+O\left((\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}\beta}{\alpha^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

By the definition of stationary measure, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla G(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right)\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\alpha\mathbf{x}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right)-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\alpha\mathbf{x}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{X}}\left(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\alpha\mathbf{x}^{t}\right)\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\eta^{2}_{\mathbf{x}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\alpha\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\hat{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\alpha\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

To guarantee
\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla G(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}
is less than
\epsilon^{2}
, we need:

T=O\left(\frac{F_{\max}}{\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\epsilon^{2}}\right)
,
\alpha=\Theta\left(\frac{\epsilon}{\mathcal{D}_{\mathcal{X}}}\right)
and

\displaystyle M

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\max\left\{\frac{L^{6}L_{f}^{2}D_{\mathcal{X}}^{4}\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{4}},1\right\}\right),B=\Theta\left(\max\left\{\frac{L^{3}L_{f}^{2}D_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{4}},1\right\}\right),

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{y}}

\displaystyle=\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{1}{\eta_{x}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}},\frac{1}{L_{f}G_{g}^{2}},\frac{\alpha^{2}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}{L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}}\right\}\right),\eta_{\mathbf{x}}=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{L^{2}}\right),

which yields the total gradient complexity:

\displaystyle O\left(\max\left\{\frac{L^{3}L_{f}^{2}D_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{4}},1\right\}\cdot\frac{F_{\max}D_{\mathcal{X}}^{2}L^{4}}{\epsilon^{4}}\right).

Appendix E
Proof of Both Sides Composition

In this section we provide the proof of results in primal and dual composition setting (Theorem
5
).

We first define some notations. Let
\mathbf{w}=[\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}]
be stacked variable.
\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{w}):=\nabla f(g(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}))\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})+\nabla h(\mathbf{x})+\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{0}\right)
,
\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{w}):=\nabla f(g(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}))\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})-\nabla r(\mathbf{y})-\frac{2}{\gamma}\left(\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{0}\right)
. Hence we define stacked variable
\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}):=[\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{w}),\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}(\mathbf{w})]
.
Similarly, we define stacked variable
\mathbf{g}^{t}:=[\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}},\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}}]
for the gradients we used in updating rule.

Proposition 2
.

For Algorithm
3
, under the assumptions of Theorem
5
, the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}\right)+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Lemma 19

(Convergence of Iterates difference)
.

For Algorithm
3
, under the assumptions of Theorem
5
, the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+4\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)G_{g}^{2}\left(\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\mathbf{w}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+2\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

For the ease of presentation, we define the following two auxiliary variables:

\displaystyle g^{t}=g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{M}^{t}),g^{t\mapsto t-1}=g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{M}^{t})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\mathcal{M}^{t}).

According to updating rule of
\mathbf{z}
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}=(1-\beta)(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1})+(1-\beta)(g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2})+\beta(g^{t}-g^{t-1}).

Taking expectation w.r.t.
\mathcal{M}^{t}
,
\mathcal{M}^{t-1}
yields:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|(1-\beta)(\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1})+(1-\beta)(g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2})+\beta(g^{t}-g^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(1)}}{{\leq}}\left(1+\frac{\beta}{2-2\beta}\right)(1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}+(g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2})\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2-2\beta}{\beta}\right)\left\|\beta(g^{t}-g^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(2)}}{{\leq}}\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)(1-\beta){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}+(g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2})\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t}-g^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(3)}}{{\leq}}\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)(1-\beta)\left(1+\frac{\beta}{2-2\beta}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)(1-\beta)\left(1+\frac{2-2\beta}{\beta}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2}\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t}-g^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(4)}}{{\leq}}\left(1-\frac{\beta}{2}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+\underbrace{\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t\mapsto t-1}-g^{t-1\mapsto t-2}\right\|^{2}}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t}-g^{t-1}\right\|^{2}}_{T_{2}},

where in (1) and (3) we use Young’s inequality that
\left\|\mathbf{a}+\mathbf{b}\right\|^{2}\leq(1+a)\left\|\mathbf{a}\right\|^{2}+(1+\frac{1}{a})\left\|\mathbf{b}\right\|^{2}
.
Now we bound
T_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle T_{1}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{M}^{t})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\mathcal{M}^{t})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+2\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1};\mathcal{M}^{t-1})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-2},\mathbf{y}^{t-2};\mathcal{M}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)G_{g}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t-2}\right\|^{2}\right),

and for
T_{2}
:

\displaystyle T_{2}\leq\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\beta^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}\right).

Putting pieces together will conclude the proof.
∎

Lemma 20
.

Let
\mathbf{w}^{*}
be the solution of
MVI(F^{\gamma},\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y})
. For Algorithm
3
, under the assumptions of Theorem
5
, the following statement holds true:

\displaystyle A_{t+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{4}\right)A_{t}+\left(8\eta^{2}+32C_{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}\right)\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2\beta^{2}C_{1}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M},

where
A_{t}={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}
,
C_{1}=220\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\max\left\{1,\frac{1}{\mu},G_{g}^{2}\right\}
, and
C_{2}=\frac{\mu}{16\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}C_{1}=14\mu\max\left\{1,\frac{1}{\mu},G_{g}^{2}\right\}
.

Proof.

Recall that Algorithm
3
is optimizing a
\mu:=\frac{1}{\gamma}-\rho
strongly convex strongly concave function
F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=F(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})+\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{0}\right\|^{2}
, and it is
\tilde{L}:=L+\frac{1}{\gamma}
smooth. According to updating rule we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\eta(\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\ \leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{2(\frac{1}{2\mu\eta}-1)}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}-\eta(\nabla F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+2(\frac{1}{2\mu\eta}-1)\right)\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}-\nabla F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-(\nabla F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\mathbf{g}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{1}{2(\frac{1}{2\mu\eta}-1)}\right)\left(1-2\eta\mu\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\eta(\nabla F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu\eta}\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}-\nabla F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-(\nabla F^{\gamma}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})-\mathbf{g}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-{\eta\mu}+\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right)+32\frac{\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\mu B},

where the last step is due to

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}\right\|^{2}=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi)-\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}}_{A}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi)-\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}}_{B}.

For
A
,

\displaystyle A\leq

\displaystyle 2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi)-\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{1}{B^{2}}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi)-\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\frac{1}{B^{2}}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Similarly
B\leq 2\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}
.
Now we examine the convergence to optimal point:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}-\eta(\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{*}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{*})\right\|^{2}-2\eta{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{*}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{*})\right\|^{2}-2\eta{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t})-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{*}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-2\eta{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\rangle.

Due to
\mu
strong monotoncity we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\eta\mu){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{*})\right\|^{2}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad-2\eta{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\eta\mu){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{*})\right\|^{2}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{\eta}{\mu}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\eta\mu\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\eta\mu+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+2\eta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{\eta}{\mu}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\eta\mu\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Now we add
C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}
on both side, and
C_{1}(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}-C_{1}(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

We apply Proposition
2
to replace
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}
:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2})+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{1}\left((1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-C_{1}(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2})+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}C_{1}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle\quad+4(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{1}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

Adding

(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}C_{1}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+4(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{1}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+2}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

We now plug in the bound for
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+2}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

:

\displaystyle A_{t+1}\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})A_{t}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}C_{1}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle+\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{1}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}G_{g}^{2}-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\left(1-{\eta\mu}+\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{4\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\right)+32\frac{\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\mu B}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})A_{t}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}C_{1}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+32\frac{C_{2}\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\mu B}

\displaystyle+\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{1}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}G_{g}^{2}+C_{2}\left(1-{\eta\mu}+\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}\right)-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{2}\frac{4\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\right).

Again applying the bound for
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})A_{t}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}C_{1}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+32\frac{C_{2}\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\mu B}

\displaystyle+\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{1}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}G_{g}^{2}+C_{2}\left(1-{\eta\mu}+\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2}\right)-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})C_{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C_{2}\frac{4\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left((1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})A_{t}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}C_{1}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+32\frac{C_{2}\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\mu B}

\displaystyle+\left(4(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}G_{g}^{2}-\left(\frac{\eta\mu}{2}-\frac{16\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}(1-\beta)^{2}\right)C_{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

We choose
\eta\leq\min\left\{\frac{\mu^{3}}{512},\frac{\mu}{8\tilde{L}^{2}}\right\}
and
(1-\beta)^{2}\leq\frac{\mu^{2}}{256G^{4}_{g}L^{2}_{f}}
, we know

\displaystyle A_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}+2\eta^{2}\tilde{L}^{2})A_{t}+8\eta^{2}\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+2\beta^{2}C_{1}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+32\frac{C_{2}\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\mu B}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}-(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{4})C_{1}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right)}_{T_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{64G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}C_{1}-\frac{\eta\mu}{4}C_{2}\right)}_{T_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

Since we choose
C_{2}=\frac{\mu}{16\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}C_{1}
, we know
T_{2}\leq 0
, together with
\eta\leq\frac{\mu^{3}}{512}
which yields:

\displaystyle A_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{4})A_{t}+\left(8\eta^{2}+32C_{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}\right)\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2\beta^{2}C_{1}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left(2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}C_{1}-(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\eta\mu}{4})C_{1}\right)}_{T_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

To ensure
T_{1}\leq 0
, we need

\displaystyle C_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\eta^{2}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\eta\mu}{4}-(1-\beta)^{2}},

It can be satisfied If we choose

\displaystyle(1-\beta)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\eta\mu}{4}-\frac{\mu}{220\max\left\{1,\frac{1}{\mu},G_{f}^{2}\right\}}

\displaystyle C_{1}

\displaystyle=220\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\max\left\{1,\frac{1}{\mu},G_{g}^{2}\right\}

and we know
T_{1}\leq 0
, which concludes the proof.

∎

Proposition 3

(Three points)
.

If
\tilde{\mathbf{w}}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{W}}(\mathbf{v}-\eta\mathbf{g})
, then the following statements holds:

\displaystyle\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g},\,\tilde{\mathbf{w}}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{v}-\tilde{\mathbf{w}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\tilde{\mathbf{w}}\right\|^{2}\leq\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g},\,\mathbf{w}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{v}\right\|^{2},\forall\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{W}.

Lemma 21
.

Define
\mathbf{u}^{t+1}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{W}}(\mathbf{w}^{t}-\eta\mathbf{g}^{t+1})
. Then for Algorithm
3
, under the assumptions of Theorem
5
, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{2\eta}{\mu}+72\eta\right)G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+\left(72\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+144\eta G_{g}^{2}G_{f}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

In Proposition
3
, we set
\tilde{\mathbf{w}}=\mathbf{w}^{t+1}
,
\mathbf{v}=\mathbf{w}^{t}
and
\mathbf{w}=\mathbf{u}^{t+1}

\displaystyle\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t},\,\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

(30)

Again in Proposition
3
, we set
\tilde{\mathbf{w}}=\mathbf{u}^{t+1}
,
\mathbf{v}=\mathbf{w}^{t}
,
\mathbf{g}=\mathbf{g}^{t+1}

\displaystyle\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}\right\rangle-\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

(31)

Adding (
30
) and (
31
) yields:

\displaystyle\eta\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle\eta\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\left\langle\eta\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}\right\rangle+\eta\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t+1}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

Setting
\mathbf{w}=\mathbf{w}^{*}
, according to
\frac{1}{\gamma}
-strongly monotone of
\mathbf{g}(\cdot)
, we have

\displaystyle\eta\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\eta\mu\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+\eta\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t+1}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

For the two inner products, we apply Cauchy-Schwartz:

\displaystyle\eta{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t+1}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\eta{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t+1}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{2}\eta\left(\frac{1}{\mu}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t+1}-\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{w}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\mu{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{2}\eta\left(\frac{1}{\mu}2G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\mu{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}\right),

and

\displaystyle\eta\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{1}{2}\eta\left(\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\eta\left(\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right).

For
\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}
we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 6\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+2})\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}}_{A}+\frac{4}{\gamma^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+6\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1})\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t}}\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\xi)\right\|^{2}}_{B}

\displaystyle+6\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+2})\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\frac{1}{B}\sum_{(\zeta,\xi)\in\mathcal{B}^{t+1}}\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+2};\zeta)\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\xi)\right\|^{2}}_{C}.

We bound above terms as follows. For A:

\displaystyle A\leq 2G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

For B and C:

\displaystyle B,C\leq\frac{2(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

Putting pieces together we know

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 12G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t+2}\right\|^{2}+12G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{4}{\gamma^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{48(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 12G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t+2}\right\|^{2}+12G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{4}{\gamma^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{48(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Hence

\displaystyle\eta\left\langle\mathbf{g}^{t}-\mathbf{g}^{t+1},\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\rangle\geq-\frac{1}{2}\eta\left(24G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t+2}\right\|^{2}+\left(24G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}+\frac{4}{\gamma^{2}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{96(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}\right).

Plugging back yields:

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\eta\left(24G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t+2}\right\|^{2}+\left(24G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}+\frac{4}{\gamma^{2}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{96(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\eta\left(\frac{1}{\mu}2G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\gamma}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\eta\frac{1}{\gamma}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle-12\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t+2}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\mu\eta}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}-\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\eta\left(24G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}+\frac{4}{\gamma^{2}}\right)\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{u}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{u}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

By our choice
\eta\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(24G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}+\frac{4}{\gamma^{2}}\right)^{-1}
, we have
\left(1-\eta\left(24G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}+\frac{4}{\gamma^{2}}\right)\right)\geq\frac{1}{2}
, which yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+12\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t+2}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+12\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left(3{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+3{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+3{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\mathbf{z}^{t+2}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left((1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(36\eta G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+2\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+\left(36\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+36\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}.

Plugging bound for
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(36\eta G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+2\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+\left(36\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+36\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\left((1-\beta)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+2\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(36\eta G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\left(2\frac{\eta}{\mu}+72\eta\right)G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle+\left(72\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+144(1-\beta)^{2}\eta G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}+(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Since we choose
\eta\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{576G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}},\frac{\mu}{64G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}}\right\}
, we know
36\eta G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}+4(1-\beta)^{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{8}
, which yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{2\eta}{\mu}+72\eta\right)G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle+\left(72\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+144\eta G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

∎

E.1
Proof of Lemma
1

Proof.

First according to [Eq.(23) of
[
22
]
],

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{W}}\left\langle\nabla F_{k}(\mathbf{w}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}\right\rangle\leq(D+\frac{1}{\eta}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

(32)

Revoking Lemma
21

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{2\eta}{\mu}+72\eta\right)G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(72\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}+144\eta G_{g}^{4}L_{f}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{2\eta}{\mu}+72\eta\right)G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}

\displaystyle\quad+220\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\max\left\{1,\frac{1}{\mu},G_{g}^{2}\right\}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

(33)

To bound
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{*}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}
, we revoke Lemma
20
:

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+2}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+C_{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+C_{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(8\eta^{2}+32C_{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}\right)\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2\beta^{2}C_{1}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}\right)^{t}\left(\left\|\mathbf{w}^{0}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{0})\right\|^{2}+C_{2}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{0}-\mathbf{w}^{-1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\eta\mu}\left(\left(8\eta^{2}+32C_{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}\right)\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2C_{1}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right).

where
C_{1}=220\eta G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\max\left\{1,\frac{1}{\mu},G_{g}^{2}\right\}
, and
C_{2}=14\mu\max\left\{1,\frac{1}{\mu},G_{g}^{2}\right\}
. Plugging above bound in (
33
) yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\left\|\mathbf{w}^{t}-\mathbf{w}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}\right)^{t}\left(\left\|\mathbf{w}^{0}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{0})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\eta\mu}\left(\left(8\eta^{2}+32C_{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}\right)\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2C_{1}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{2\eta}{\mu}+72\eta\right)G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

Combining with (
32
) yields:

\displaystyle\max_{\mathbf{w}\in\mathcal{W}}\left\langle\nabla F_{k}(\mathbf{w}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{w}^{t+1}-\mathbf{w}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left(D+\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\left(1-\frac{\eta\mu}{2}\right)^{t}\left(\left\|\mathbf{w}^{0}-\mathbf{w}^{*}\right\|^{2}+C_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0},\mathbf{y}^{0})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(D+\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\frac{2}{\eta\mu}\left(\left(8\eta^{2}+32C_{2}\frac{\eta}{\mu}\right)\frac{(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}+\left(2C_{1}+C_{2}\frac{8\eta}{\mu}G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\right)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(D+\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\left(\frac{2\eta}{\mu}+72\eta\right)G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}+\left(D+\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\frac{48\eta(G_{g}^{2}+G_{f}^{2})\sigma^{2}}{B}.

To ensure RHS is less than
\epsilon
, we need
t\geq\Omega(\frac{1}{\eta\mu}\ln(\frac{D+1/\eta}{\epsilon}))
and
M=B\geq\Omega\left((D+\frac{1}{\eta})\frac{G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\mu\epsilon}\max\left\{1,\frac{1}{\mu},G_{g}^{2}\right\})\right)
, which yields the gradient complexity of

\displaystyle O\left(\left(D+\frac{1}{\eta}\right)\frac{L^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\mu^{2}\eta\epsilon}\log\left(\frac{D+1/\eta}{\epsilon}\right)\right).

∎

E.2
Proof of Theorem
5

Proof.

Let
\bar{\mathbf{w}}
be the solution of SVI induced by
F^{\gamma}_{\mathbf{w}}:=F(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime})+\frac{1}{2\gamma}(\mathbf{w}^{\prime}-\mathbf{w})
. Let
\mathbf{w}_{k^{*}}=(\mathbf{x}_{k^{*}},\mathbf{y}_{k^{*}})
be the solution returned by Algorithm
4
.
According to [Eq.(21)
[
22
]
], if Algorithm
3
returns a
\tilde{\epsilon}
-accurate solution, then we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{k^{*}}-\bar{\mathbf{w}}_{k^{*}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{2D\theta_{K}}{\sum_{k=0}^{K}\theta_{k}}+\frac{4c\tilde{\epsilon}}{\rho}.

Since we choose
\theta_{k}=(k+1)^{\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{k^{*}}-\bar{\mathbf{w}}_{k^{*}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{2D(\alpha+1)}{K}+\frac{4c\tilde{\epsilon}}{\rho}.

According to [Lemma 6
[
22
]
], we know that if
\left\|\mathbf{w}-\bar{\mathbf{w}}\right\|\leq\gamma\epsilon
, then

\displaystyle dist\left(0,\partial(F(\bar{\mathbf{x}},\bar{\mathbf{y}})+\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}}(\bar{\mathbf{x}},\bar{\mathbf{y}}))\right)\leq\epsilon

To ensure
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{w}_{k^{*}}-\bar{\mathbf{w}}_{k^{*}}\right\|^{2}
is less than
\gamma^{2}\epsilon^{2}
where
\gamma=\frac{1}{2\rho}
, we need
K\geq\frac{16\rho^{2}D(\alpha+1)}{\epsilon^{2}}
, and
\tilde{\epsilon}\leq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{32\rho c}
, together by our choice
\eta=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{L^{2}}\right)
which yields the gradient complexity of

\displaystyle O\left(\left(D+L^{2}\right)\frac{\rho^{2}DL^{4}\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{4}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\epsilon}\right)\right).

∎

Appendix F
Proof of Varaince Reduction Algorithm

In this Section we will provide proofs for Theorem
6
and
7
.

In the proof we will use the following notations:

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})=F(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})+\frac{\mu_{x}+L}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}=\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t})+(\mu_{x}+L)(\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{0}),

\displaystyle\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}}=\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),

For notational convenience, we define
\tilde{\sigma}^{2}=\max\left\{\sigma^{2},2(G_{f}^{2}+G_{g}^{2})\sigma^{2}\right\}
.

The following Lemma shows that the tracking error for inner function is reduced via our variance reduction technique.

Lemma 22

(Tracking error with variance reduction)
.

For Algorithm
5
, under assumptions of Theorem
6
, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\beta\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1+\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\left(6(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+3\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B_{\tau}}+\frac{G_{g}^{2}}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1}\right\|^{2}\right).

where
t_{p}
is the last iteration
t
such that
t\mod\tau=0
.

Proof.

Recall the updating rule:

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}^{t+1}=(1-\beta)(\mathbf{z}^{t}+g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\mathcal{I}^{t}_{\mathbf{z}})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\mathcal{I}^{t}_{\mathbf{z}}))+\beta g^{t-1},

where
g^{t}=g^{t-1}+g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\mathcal{I}^{t}_{\mathbf{z}})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\mathcal{I}^{t}_{\mathbf{z}})
and every
\tau
iterations, we use large mini-batch to update
g^{t}
:
g^{t}=g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{z}})
. We hence have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t-1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})+\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\xi\in\mathcal{I}^{t}_{\mathbf{z}}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\xi)-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\xi)+g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t-1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\frac{1}{B}\sum_{\xi\in\mathcal{I}^{t}_{\mathbf{z}}}g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\xi)-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\xi)+g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t-1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{B^{2}}\sum_{\xi\in\mathcal{I}^{t}_{\mathbf{z}}}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\xi^{t})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\xi^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t-1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{G_{g}^{2}}{B}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t_{p}}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t_{p}})\right\|^{2}+\frac{G_{g}^{2}}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B_{\tau}}+\frac{G_{g}^{2}}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1}\right\|^{2}.

Again by the updating rule we have:

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})=(1-\beta)(\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}))+(1-\beta)(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t}))+\beta(g^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t}))+(1-\beta)(g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\xi^{t})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\xi^{t})).

Taking expected norm on both sides and applying Young’s inequality yields:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{(1+\beta)(1-\beta)^{2}}_{\leq 1-\beta}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{\beta}){\mathbb{E}}\left\|(1-\beta)(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t}))+\beta(g^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t}))+(1-\beta)(g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\xi^{t})-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\xi^{t}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\beta\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{\beta})\left(6(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+3\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|g^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\beta\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{1}{\beta})\left(6(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+3\beta^{2}\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B_{\tau}}\right)+\frac{G_{g}^{2}}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1}\right\|^{2}\right).

∎

Lemma 23

(Gradient bounds)
.

For Algorithm
5
, under assumptions of Theorem
6
, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}+\frac{1}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}\left(2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}+\frac{1}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t}L^{2}_{f}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right).

Proof.

By updating rule in Algorithm
5
we have:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}+G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}})-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}})-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}+\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}+G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}})-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}})-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}})-(\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}))\right\|^{2},

where the last step is due to
E[\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}})-(\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}))]=0
. We now turn to bounding the variance term.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}})-(\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{B^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{B}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\zeta_{i},\xi_{i})-(\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\zeta_{i},\xi_{i}))\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{B^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{B}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t-1},\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\zeta_{i},\xi_{i})-G^{k}_{\mathbf{x}}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\zeta_{i},\xi_{i})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{B^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{B}\left\|\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\zeta_{i})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\xi_{i})-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\zeta_{i})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\xi_{i})\right\|^{2}.

By simple variance decomposition we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\zeta_{i})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1};\xi_{i})-\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\zeta_{i})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t};\xi_{i})\right\|^{2}\leq 2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\left\|(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

Hence

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t-1}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{B}\left(2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t_{p}}_{\mathbf{x}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t_{p}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}\left(2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right),

where
t_{p}
denotes the last iteration such that
t\mod\tau=0
. From the updating rule we know
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{g}^{t_{p}}_{\mathbf{x}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t_{p}}_{\mathbf{x}}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}
.
Similarly,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t+1}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|G_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{z}^{t+2},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}})-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}-(G_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t};\mathcal{B}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}})-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{B^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{B}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t};\zeta_{i})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1};\zeta_{i})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t}_{\mathbf{y}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{B}L^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t})-(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t_{p}}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}^{t_{p}}_{\mathbf{y}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t}L^{2}_{f}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}+\frac{1}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t}L^{2}_{f}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right).

∎

Lemma 24
.

For Algorithm
5
, under assumptions of Theorem
6
, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2},

where
L_{f}^{\prime}:=L_{f}+\mu_{x}+L
.

Proof.

Define
\phi(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})=h(\mathbf{x}^{t})+\nabla_{1}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})g(\mathbf{x}^{t})
,
f_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}):=f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2}
. According to updating rule we have:

\displaystyle\mathbf{x}^{t+1}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{x}^{t}-\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}=\arg\min_{\mathbf{x}}h(\mathbf{x})+\phi(\mathbf{x})-\phi(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}\phi(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle.

Then according to [Lemma 7.1
[
10
]
] we have:

\displaystyle h(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})+\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t},\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\rangle\leq h(\mathbf{x})+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right),

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t},\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle\leq r(\mathbf{y})+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right).

Re-arranging terms yields:

\displaystyle h(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})+

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\rangle\leq h(\mathbf{x})+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}},

(34)

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})+\theta(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad\leq r(\mathbf{y})+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}},

(35)

where

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}=\left\langle\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}=\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})+\theta(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}))-\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t},\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle.

The rest of the proof follows [Lemma 13
[
45
]
]. Using
\mu_{x}
-convexity of
f_{k}
w.r.t.
\mathbf{x}
, we can lower bound the inner product in (
34
) as

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\geq f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-f_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle.

Putting pieces together yields:

\displaystyle h(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})+f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-

\displaystyle f_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+r(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-r(\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})+\theta(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+h(\mathbf{x})+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}.

Adding
f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})
on both sides yields:

\displaystyle f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+h(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})-

\displaystyle f_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+r(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-r(\mathbf{y})-h(\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})+(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}.

Due to
L_{f}^{\prime}:=L_{f}+\mu_{x}+L
smoothness of
f_{k}
, we know
f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\rangle\leq\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}
. Hence we have

\displaystyle f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+h(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})-

\displaystyle f_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+r(\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-r(\mathbf{y})-h(\mathbf{x})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})+(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}.

Adding
f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})
on both sides yields:

\displaystyle\underbrace{f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})+h(\mathbf{x}^{t+1})-r(\mathbf{y})-(h(\mathbf{x})+f_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-r(\mathbf{y}^{t+1}))}_{=F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})+(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}.

Due to
\mu_{y}
strong concavity of
f_{k}
w.r.t.
\mathbf{y}
, we have
f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\leq\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\rangle-\frac{\mu_{y}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}
. So we have

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\leq

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\rangle-\frac{\mu_{y}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}+\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})+(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}.

Notice the following identity:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1}),\,\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})+(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=
