\displaystyle-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle.

We thus arrive at

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{\mu_{y}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}+\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}.

Now it remains to bound
\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}
and
\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}
. For
\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}
:

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}+\mu_{x}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}\right).

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{x}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\nabla f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\nabla f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})\nabla g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}.

Hence we have

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}.

Similarly we have for
\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{t+1}_{\mathbf{y}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{y}}\left\|2\mathbf{q}^{t}-\mathbf{q}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-(\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}))\right\|^{2}+\mu_{y}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu_{y}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\|^{2}+\frac{4}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu_{y}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\|^{2}+\frac{8}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{8}{\mu_{y}}L^{2}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\mu_{y}}L^{2}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}.

Putting pieces together will conclude the proof.

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}.

∎

Lemma 25

(Primal-dual gap convergence)
.

For Algorithm
5
, under assumptions of Theorem
6
, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[F_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1}))-F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{*}(\mathbf{y}_{k+1}),\mathbf{y}_{k+1})]\leq

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1})-\mathbf{y}_{k}\right\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T}+\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{y}_{k+1})-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2}}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}+\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}},

where
C=\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{12L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}
,
\mathbf{x}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{y})=\arg\min_{\mathbf{x}\in\mathcal{X}}F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
and
\mathbf{y}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{x})=\arg\max_{\mathbf{y}\in\mathcal{Y}}F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
.

Proof.

Due to Lemma
24
we have

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t+1}),\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}.

Define
\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}=F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+C\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right)
, and
\Gamma^{t}=\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t}-\mathbf{y}\right\rangle
. Adding
C\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right)
on both side yields:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}\leq-

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t+1}+\Gamma^{t}+\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{L_{f}^{\prime}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{8}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t+1},\mathbf{y}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t-1}-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(\mathbf{z}^{t},\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right).

Notice that by Cauchy-schwartz and AM-GM inequality we have:

\displaystyle\left\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1}),\,\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\left\|\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-\nabla_{\mathbf{y}}f(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}\left\|(g(\mathbf{x}^{t}),\mathbf{y}^{t})-(g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1}),\mathbf{y}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}\left(G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}.

Plugging back yields:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}\leq-

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t+1}+\Gamma^{t}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{L^{\prime}_{f}}{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{x}}\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{8}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{2}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t-1}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right).

Plugging in the bound from Lemma
23
for
\left\|\mathbf{g}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{x}}^{t}\right\|^{2}
and
\left\|\mathbf{q}^{t}-\bar{\mathbf{g}}_{\mathbf{y}}^{t}\right\|^{2}
yields:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}\leq

\displaystyle-\Gamma^{t+1}+\Gamma^{t}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{L^{\prime}_{f}}{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{1}{\mu_{x}B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}\left(2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\frac{8}{\mu_{y}B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}L^{2}_{f}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{\mu_{y}B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-2}L^{2}_{f}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+C\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right).

Since
\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-2}L^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)\leq\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}L^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)
, and
\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\leq 3\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+3G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+3\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t})-\mathbf{z}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}\leq-

\displaystyle\Gamma^{t+1}+\Gamma^{t}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{t+1}\right\|^{2}\right)-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{L^{\prime}_{f}}{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{x}B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}\left(3\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}+3G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+3\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1})-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{x}B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}\left(2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10}{\mu_{y}B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}L^{2}_{f}\left(3\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1})\right\|^{2}+3G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}+3\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}}{\mu_{x}}\right)\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+C\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right).

Summing from
t=0
to
T-1
yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma^{0}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{T}\right\|^{2}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2T}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{T}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t-1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{L^{\prime}_{f}}{2}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}}{\mu_{x}B}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{t^{\prime}=c(t)}^{t-1}\left(3\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}})\right\|^{2}+3G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+3\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1})-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mu_{x}B}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{t^{\prime}=c(t)}^{t-1}\left(2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+2}\right\|^{2}+2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{10}{\mu_{y}B}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\sum_{t^{\prime}=c(t)}^{t-1}L^{2}_{f}\left(3\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1})\right\|^{2}+3G_{g}^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}+3\left\|g(\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}})-\mathbf{z}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{y}^{t^{\prime}+1}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}+\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+C\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}\right).

Re-arranging terms yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}\leq

\displaystyle\Gamma^{0}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{T}\right\|^{2}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2T}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{T}\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}-\frac{2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}-\frac{10\tau L^{2}}{\mu_{y}B}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{L^{\prime}_{f}}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}-\frac{6G_{g}^{4}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}-\frac{2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}-\frac{30G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{y}B}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+C\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle-\left(C-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}.

Plugging the bound for
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}
yields:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+C\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-\left(C-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu_{y}}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+C\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}

\displaystyle\times\left(\left(1-\beta\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}+\left(1+\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\left(6(1-\beta)^{2}G_{g}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t-1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}+3\beta^{2}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B_{\tau}}+\frac{G_{g}^{2}}{B}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{p}}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}^{t^{\prime}-1}\right\|^{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-\left(C-\frac{2L^{2}}{\mu_{y}}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+C\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(1-\beta\right){\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\left(\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+C\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\beta}\right)\left(6G_{g}^{2}+\frac{\tau G_{g}^{2}}{B}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t}-\mathbf{x}^{t-1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+C\right)\left(1+\frac{1}{\beta}\right)3\frac{\sigma^{2}}{B_{\tau}}-\left(C-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\right)\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2}.

We choose
\beta=\frac{1}{2}
, and
C=\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{12L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}
, so we have:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{8L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}+C\right)\left(1-\beta\right)-\left(C-\frac{2L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}\right)=0.

Define
\Delta_{\mathbf{x}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{t}\right\|^{2}
and
\Delta_{\mathbf{y}}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{t+1}-\mathbf{y}^{t}\right\|^{2}
, and we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}\leq\Gamma^{0}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{T}\right\|^{2}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2T}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{T}\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{y}}}-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}-\frac{2G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}-\frac{10\tau L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}B}\right)\Delta_{\mathbf{y}}

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}-\frac{L^{\prime}_{f}}{2}-\eta_{\mathbf{y}}L_{f}^{2}G_{g}^{2}-\frac{6G_{g}^{4}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}-\frac{2G_{f}^{2}L_{g}^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}-\frac{30G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{y}B}-\left(\frac{72G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{360\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{60L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{6L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)(6+\frac{\tau}{B})G_{g}^{2}\right)\Delta_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}.

We choose
\eta_{\mathbf{y}}\leq\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{1}{L_{f}}\right\},\frac{\mu_{y}B}{\tau G_{g}^{2}L_{f}^{2}}\right)
and
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{\mu_{y}B}{G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau},\frac{1}{L^{\prime}_{f}},\frac{\rho B^{2}}{G_{g}^{4}L^{2}_{f}\tau^{2}},\frac{\mu_{y}B^{2}}{\tau^{2}}\right\}\right)
which yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}\leq

\displaystyle\Gamma^{0}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T}\left(\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{T}\right\|^{2}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}-\frac{\mu_{x}}{2T}\right)\left(\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{0}\right\|^{2}-\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{T}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{t+1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{x})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+C({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t+1}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{t}-g(\mathbf{x}^{t-1})\right\|^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{x})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+\frac{1}{T}C({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{T}-g(\mathbf{x}^{T-1})\right\|^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{-1})\right\|^{2})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{x})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})-\frac{1}{T}C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{-1})\right\|^{2}.

Since we choose
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\frac{1}{2L}
and by convention
\mathbf{x}^{-1}=\mathbf{x}^{0}
, we have

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}F_{k}(\mathbf{x}^{t+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}^{t+1})+O\left(\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{y}^{0}\right\|^{2}}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T}+\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}^{0}\right\|^{2}}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}+O\left(\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}\right)+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}.

∎

Lemma 26
.

For Algorithm
5
, under assumptions of Theorem
6
, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle\left(Q-1\right)

\displaystyle\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})\leq\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k},\mathbf{y}_{k})

\displaystyle+Q\left(\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+O\left(\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}\right)+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}\right),

where
Q=\frac{\min\left\{\frac{\mu_{x}}{4},\frac{\mu_{y}}{4}\right\}}{\max\left\{\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T},\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}\right\}}
.

Proof.

Due to [Lemma. 1
[
38
]
]

\displaystyle\frac{\mu_{x}}{4}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{y})-\mathbf{x}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{y}}{4}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{x})-\mathbf{y}^{\prime}\right\|^{2}\leq\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})+\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}^{\prime},\mathbf{y}^{\prime}).

We set
\mathbf{x}=\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}=\mathbf{y}_{k+1}
,
\mathbf{x}^{\prime}=\mathbf{x}_{k},\mathbf{y}^{\prime}=\mathbf{y}_{k}
, which yields

\displaystyle\frac{\mu_{x}}{4}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{y}_{k+1})-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2}+\frac{\mu_{y}}{4}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1})-\mathbf{y}_{k}\right\|^{2}\leq\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})+\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k},\mathbf{y}_{k}).

(36)

Due to Lemma
25
we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})\leq

\displaystyle\max\left\{\frac{1}{2\eta_{\mathbf{y}}T},\frac{1}{4\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T}\right\}\left({\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{y}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1})-\mathbf{y}_{k}\right\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{x}^{*}_{k}(\mathbf{y}_{k+1})-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2}\right)+\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}.

(37)

Combining (
36
) and (
37
) yields:

\displaystyle Q\left(\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})-\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}-\frac{10\sigma^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}-\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}-\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})+\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k},\mathbf{y}_{k})

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle\left(Q-1\right)\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})\leq\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k},\mathbf{y}_{k})

\displaystyle\qquad+Q\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\sigma^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}\right).

∎

Lemma 27

(Lemma 8
[
38
]
)
.

If
F(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
is
\rho
weakly convex in
\mathbf{x}
, and let
F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})=F(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})+\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2}
. Then the following statements hold true for any
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}\in(0,1)
:

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{\rho}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}-1\right)\right)\left(F_{k+1}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k+1}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{2}\frac{\alpha_{1}}{1-\alpha_{1}}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k+1}-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2}

(38)

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})

\displaystyle\geq\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{k+1})-\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{k})+\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k+1}-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2}

(39)

\displaystyle F_{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y})-F_{k}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\rho\alpha_{2}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k}-\mathbf{x}_{k}^{*}\right\|^{2}-\frac{\rho\alpha_{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{2})}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k}-\mathbf{x}_{k+1}\right\|^{2}.

(40)

Lemma 28
.

For Algorithm
5
, under assumptions of Theorem
6
, the following statement holds:

\displaystyle(Q-1)

\displaystyle\frac{a_{3}\rho\alpha_{2}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k}-\mathbf{x}_{k}^{*}\right\|^{2}\leq\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k},\mathbf{y}_{k})-(Q-1)a_{1}\left(1-\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{\rho}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}-1\right)\right)\mathrm{Gap}^{k+1}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})

\displaystyle\quad-(Q-1)a_{2}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{k+1})-\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{k})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+Q\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\sigma^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}\right),

where
a_{1},a_{2},a_{3}
are such that

\displaystyle a_{1}+a_{2}+a_{3}

\displaystyle=1,

\displaystyle a_{1}\frac{(\mu_{x}+\rho)\alpha_{1}}{2(1-\alpha_{1})}-a_{2}\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{2}+a_{3}\frac{\rho\alpha_{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{2})}

\displaystyle\leq 0,

\displaystyle(Q-1)a_{1}\left(1-\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{\rho}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}-1\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq 1.

Proof.

Due to Lemma
27
,
a_{1}\cdot(\ref{eq:three inequalities 1})+a_{2}\cdot(\ref{eq:three inequalities 2})+a_{3}\cdot(\ref{eq:three inequalities 3})
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{a_{3}\rho\alpha_{2}}{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k}-\mathbf{x}_{k}^{*}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})-a_{1}\left(1-\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{\rho}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}-1\right)\right)\mathrm{Gap}^{k+1}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})

\displaystyle\quad-a_{2}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{k+1})-\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{k})\right)+\left(a_{1}\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{2}\frac{\alpha_{1}}{1-\alpha_{1}}+a_{3}\frac{\rho\alpha_{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{2})}-a_{2}\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{2}\right)\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k+1}-\mathbf{x}_{k}\right\|^{2},

Multiply both sides with
Q-1
, plugging result from Lemma
26
, then the proof immediately follows the condition
a_{1}\frac{(\mu_{x}+\rho)\alpha_{1}}{2(1-\alpha_{1})}-a_{2}\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{2}+a_{3}\frac{\rho\alpha_{2}}{2(1-\alpha_{2})}\leq 0
and
(Q-1)a_{1}\left(1-\frac{\mu_{x}+\rho}{\rho}\left(\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}-1\right)\right)\geq 1
.
∎

F.1
Proof of Theorem
6

Proof.

First in Lemma
28
, we set
\mu_{x}=\rho=L
, and we choose
\eta_{\mathbf{x}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\eta_{\mathbf{y}}
, together with assumption that
L\geq\mu_{y}
, we know
Q=\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}
. We set:

\displaystyle a_{1}=\frac{1}{12},a_{2}=\frac{10}{12},a_{3}=\frac{1}{12},\alpha_{1}=\frac{3}{4},\alpha_{2}=\frac{3}{4},\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}\geq 37

(41)

, the condition in Lemma
28
can hold, and we have:

\displaystyle(\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}-1)\frac{3L}{96}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k}-\mathbf{x}_{k}^{*}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{k},\mathbf{y}_{k})-\mathrm{Gap}^{k+1}(\mathbf{x}_{k+1},\mathbf{y}_{k+1})

\displaystyle\quad-(\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}-1)\frac{5}{6}\left(\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{k+1})-\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{k})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}\left(\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{10\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\left(\frac{24G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{120\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{20L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{2L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)9\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}\right),

Due to the fact
4L^{2}\left\|\mathbf{x}_{k}-\mathbf{x}_{k}^{*}\right\|^{2}=\left\|\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}_{k})\right\|^{2}
, and summing over
k=0
to
K-1
we have

\displaystyle(\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}-1)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\frac{3L}{384L^{2}}\left\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}_{k})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{0},\mathbf{y}_{0})}{K}+(\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}-1)\frac{5(\Phi(\mathbf{x}_{0})-\min_{\mathbf{x}\in\mathcal{X}}\Phi(\mathbf{x}))}{6K}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}O\left(\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\left(\frac{G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)\frac{\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}\right),

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=0}^{K-1}\left\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2L}(\mathbf{x}_{k})\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{384L\mathrm{Gap}^{k}(\mathbf{x}_{0},\mathbf{y}_{0})}{3(\eta_{\mathbf{x}}T\mu_{y}-1)K}+\frac{1920L\Delta_{\Phi}}{18K}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{C{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}}{T}+\frac{L\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{x}B_{\tau}}+\frac{L\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{\mu_{y}B_{\tau}}+\left(\frac{G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}\right)\frac{L\tilde{\sigma}^{2}}{B_{\tau}}\right).

Recall that by our initialization method,
{\mathbb{E}}\left\|\mathbf{z}^{0}-g(\mathbf{x}^{0})\right\|^{2}\leq\delta
.
To ensure RHS is less than
\epsilon^{2}
, we choose
K=\Theta\left(\frac{L\Delta_{\Phi}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)
,
B_{\tau}=\Theta\left(\frac{L^{2}\sigma^{2}}{\mu_{y}\epsilon^{2}}\right)
,
\tau=\sqrt{\frac{B_{\tau}}{\kappa}}
and
B=\sqrt{\kappa B_{\tau}}
.

It remains to determine value of
T
. According to condition
41
, we need
T\geq\frac{6}{\mu_{y}\eta_{\mathbf{x}}}
. Since

\displaystyle\eta_{\mathbf{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{\mu_{y}B}{G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}\tau},\frac{1}{L^{\prime}_{f}},\frac{\rho B^{2}}{G_{g}^{4}L^{2}_{f}\tau^{2}},\frac{\mu_{y}B^{2}}{\tau^{2}}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{\mu_{y}\kappa}{G_{g}^{2}L^{2}_{f}},\frac{1}{L},\frac{\rho\kappa^{2}}{G_{g}^{4}L^{2}_{f}},\mu_{y}\kappa^{2}\right\}\right)=\Theta\left(\frac{1}{L}\right),

we know that
T\geq\Omega(\kappa)
. And hence we should choose
\delta\leq O(\kappa\epsilon^{2}/C)=O(\epsilon^{2}/L)
, since
C=\frac{12G_{g}^{2}L^{2}\tau}{\mu_{x}B}+\frac{60\tau}{\mu_{y}B}+\frac{12L^{2}_{f}}{\mu_{y}}+\frac{L_{f}^{2}G^{2}_{g}}{\mu_{x}}=O(\kappa L)
. The final gradient complexity is

\displaystyle\frac{TKB_{\tau}}{\tau}+TKB=O\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\sqrt{L}\Delta_{\Phi}\sigma}{\epsilon^{3}}\right).

∎

F.2
Proof of Theorem
7

Let
\tilde{\Phi}(\mathbf{x})=\max_{\mathbf{y}\in\mathcal{Y}}\tilde{F}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
, and
\tilde{F}(\mathbf{x},\mathbf{y})
is defined in (
2
) .
According to
[
45
]
Lemma 27, a
\epsilon/2\sqrt{6}
stationary point of Moreau Envelope of
\tilde{\Phi}(\mathbf{x})
is
\epsilon
stationary point of that of
\Phi(\mathbf{x})
. Then the complexity result follows immediately by setting
\mu=\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{LD^{2}_{\mathcal{Y}}}
in Theorem
6
.
